Backtests

Every number here is out-of-sample: each game was predicted only from games played before it, and scored against what happened and against the closing prices (Pinnacle where available). Lower log loss is better. Data: football-data.co.uk, Premier League, La Liga, Bundesliga, Serie A, Ligue 1, 2023-24 to 2025-26.
